struct VSOut { float4 pos : SV_POSITION; float2 uv : TEXCOORD0; };
VSOut vs_main(uint id : SV_VertexID)
    VSOut o;
    float2 uv = float2((id << 1) & 2, id & 2);
    o.uv  = uv;
    o.pos = float4(uv * float2(2.0, -2.0) + float2(-1.0, 1.0), 0.0, 1.0);
    return o;
Texture2D    src : register(t0);
SamplerState smp : register(s0);
cbuffer Params : register(b0) { float2 texel; float2 dir; };
// 4 bilinear taps = 16 texel box, keeps the downsample smooth.
float4 ps_down(VSOut i) : SV_TARGET
    float2 o = texel * 0.5;
    float3 c = src.Sample(smp, i.uv + float2(-o.x, -o.y)).rgb
             + src.Sample(smp, i.uv + float2( o.x, -o.y)).rgb
             + src.Sample(smp, i.uv + float2(-o.x,  o.y)).rgb
             + src.Sample(smp, i.uv + float2( o.x,  o.y)).rgb;
    return float4(c * 0.25, 1.0);
// 9-tap gaussian folded into 5 bilinear fetches per axis.
float4 ps_blur(VSOut i) : SV_TARGET
    const float w0 = 0.2270270270;
    const float w1 = 0.3162162162, o1 = 1.3846153846;
    const float w2 = 0.0702702703, o2 = 3.2307692308;
    float2 d = dir * texel;
    float3 c = src.Sample(smp, i.uv).rgb * w0;
    c += src.Sample(smp, i.uv + d * o1).rgb * w1;
    c += src.Sample(smp, i.uv - d * o1).rgb * w1;
    c += src.Sample(smp, i.uv + d * o2).rgb * w2;
    c += src.Sample(smp, i.uv - d * o2).rgb * w2;
    return float4(c, 1.0);
